(i-ḷ) āṟeṉṉumeṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukaram, neṭumutal kuṟuki aṟuveṉa muṟṟukaramāy niṟṟaliṉ, tāṉēṟiya meyyākiya ṟakaravoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟpa muṟṟukaramākiya īṟu keṭṭu muṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) aṟāyiram eṉavarum.
muṉṉar `neṭumutal kuṟukum (eḻuttu - 440) eṉṟa vitippaṭi āṟu aṟu eṉat tirintu niṟkiṉṟa muṟṟukarattaiyē īṇṭu `āṟaṉ maruṅkiṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟār āciriyar. iṅṅaṉam kūṟiyatu tirintataṉ tiripu atuveṉṉuṅ karuttiṉā leṉpar uraiyāciriyar. eṉavē īṇṭuk keṭumeṉac collappaṭṭatu aṟu eṉpataṉ kaṇ muṟṟiyalukaramē yeṉpatum āṟu eṉpataṉīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramē yeṉpatum āṟu eṉpataṉīṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramāyiṉ meyyīṟu pōlak keṭātu niṉṟu uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukku meṉpatum pulaṉām. itu puṇariyalil `kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟeṉamoḻipa eṉṉuñ cūttiravuraiyil viḷakkap peṟṟamai nōkkattakkatākum.
		oṉpā ṉiṟuti urupu tiriyā
tiṉpeṟal vēṇṭuñ cāriyai marapē.			(tol. 470)
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉpateṉṉum eṇṇiṉiṟutik kuṟṟukaram taṉ vaṭivu keṭātu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭital vēṇṭum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) oṉpatiṉāyiram eṉavarum.
		nūṟā yiramuṉ varūuṅ  kālai
nūṟa ṉiyaṟkai mutaṉilaik kiḷavi.			(tol. 471)
iḵtu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu nūṟeṉṉu meṇ aṭaiyaṭutta āyiram muṭiyumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟāyirameṉṉum aṭaiyaṭutta moḻi oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷmuṉ varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu oṉṟeṉṉu mutaṉilaikkiḷavi nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇōṭu puṇarntāṟpōla vikārameyti muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) oru nūṟāyiram eṉavarum.
		nūṟeṉ kiḷavi oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟku
īṟu ciṉaiyoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikumē.		(tol. 472)
itu nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉṉu meṇṇup peyar oṉṟumutaloṉ pāṉkaḷōṭu puṇarumiṭattu īṟāki niṉṟa kuṟṟukaran taṉṉāṟ paṟṟap paṭṭa meyyōṭuṅ keṭātu niṟpa acciṉaikku iṉamākiya ṟakaravoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nūṟṟoṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, āṟu, ēḻu, eṭṭu, oṉpatu eṉavarum.
		avaiyūr pattiṉum attoḻiṟ ṟākum.			(tol. 473)
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉpatu niṉṟu muṟkūṟiya oṉṟu mutaloṉ pāṉkaḷai yūrntu vanta patteṉpataṉōṭu puṇarumiṭattum muṟ kūṟiyavāṟu īṟuciṉai yoḻiya iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
nūṟṟorupaḵtu, irupaḵtu, muppaḵtu, nāṟpaḵtu, aimpaḵtu, aṟupaḵtu, eḻupaḵtu eṉavarum.
`ākum eṉṟataṉāl oru nūṟṟārupaḵtu, iru nūṟṟorupaḵtu eṉa nilaimoḻi aṭaiyaṭuttu muṭiyum muṭipuṅ koḷḷappaṭum.
	 	aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyātu
kuṟṟiyalukara mum valleḻut tiyaṟkaiyum 
muṟkiḷan taṉṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 474)
(i-ḷ) nūṟeṉpataṉōṭu aḷavup peyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarumiṭattu muṟkūṟiya iyalpiṟ ṟiriyātu iṉavoṟṟu mikku muṭiyum. avviṭattuk kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭāmaiyum iṉavoṟṟu mikku vaṉṟoṭar moḻiyāy niṟṟaliṉ valleḻuttu mikum iyalpum `valloṟṟut toṭarmoḻi valleḻuttu mikumē (226) eṉa vaṉṟoṭar moḻikkuk kūṟiya taṉmaiyavāy muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m) nūṟṟukkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
`tiriyā eṉṟataṉāṉ oru nūṟṟukkalam, iru nūṟṟukkalam eṉa nūṟeṉpatu aṭaiyaṭutta vaḻiyum ivviti koḷḷappaṭum.
		oṉṟumuta lākiya pattūr kiḷavi
oṉṟumuta loṉpāṟ koṟṟiṭai mikumē
niṉṟa āytaṅ keṭutal vēṇṭum.			(tol. 475)
iḵtu oṉṟumutal eṭṭu īṟākiya eṇkaḷ aṭaiyaṭutta pattaṉōṭum oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷaip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟu mutaleṭṭīṟākap patteṉṉumeṇ ēṟi oru collāki niṉṟa orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇṇup peyarkaḷ oṉṟu mutalākiya oṉpateṇkaḷ varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattup paḵtu eṉpataṉ āytaṅkeṭṭu āṇṭu iṉavoṟṟākiya oru takaravoṟṟu iṭaiyilē mikku muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupattoṉṟu, irupattoṉṟu eṉa ellāvaṟṟōṭum varum.
itaṉai naṉṉūlār,
		orupaḵtāti muṉ ṉoṉṟu mutaloṉpāṉ
eṇṇum avaiyūr piṟavu meytiṉ
āyta maḻiyavāṇ ṭākun tavvē.			   (naṉ. 196)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kūṟiṉār.
		āyiram variṉē iṉṉeṉ cāriyai
āvayi ṉoṟṟiṭai mikuta lillai.			(tol. 476)
(i-ḷ) orupaḵtu mutaliyaṉa āyirattōṭu puṇarumiṭattu iṉcāriyaipeṟum. avviṭattut takara voṟṟu iṭai mikātu eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupatiṉāyiram, irupatiṉāyiram, eṇpatiṉāyiram eṉavarum.
		aḷavum niṟaiyum āyiyal tiriyā			(tol. 477)
iḵtu oṉṟu mutalākiya pattu ūr kiḷavi muṉṉar aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) orupaḵtu mutaliya eṇkaḷiṉ muṉṉar aḷavup peyarum niṟaippeyarum vantāl oṟṟu iṭaimikātu iṉcāriyai peṟṟu muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orupatiṉkalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum, kaḻañcu, toṭi, palam eṉavum varum.
`tiriyā eṉṟataṉāl orupatiṟṟukkalam, irupatiṟṟuk kalam eṉṉum muṭipiṟku iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram iraṭṭiya ṟakara mātalum, orupatiṉāḻi eṉṉum muṭipiṉkaṇ varumoḻi nakaram tirintavaḻi nilaimoḻiyiṉ ṉakarakkēṭumkoḷka eṉpar iḷampūraṇar.
		mutaṉilai eṇṇiṉmuṉ valleḻuttu variṉum
ñanamat tōṉṟiṉum yavavan tiyaiyiṉum
mutaṉilai yiyaṟkai eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 478)
iḵtu oṉṟumutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaraip puṇarkkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟeṉṉumeṇṇiṉ muṉ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻivariṉum ña, na, ma, kkaḷākiya melleḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi variṉum yavakkaḷākiya iṭaiyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻivariṉum avvoṉṟumuta loṉpāṉkaḷ muṉ eytiya muṭipu nilaimai eyti muṭiyumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar eṉṟavāṟu.
(u-m)
orukal, cuṉai, tuṭi, paṟai, ñāṇ, nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉavarum.
		ataṉilai uyirkkum yāvaru kālaiyum
mutaṉilai okaram ōvākummē
rakarat tukaran tuvarak keṭumē.			(tol. 479)
oṉṟu mutal oṉpāṉkaḷōṭu poruṭpeyaruḷ uyir mutal moḻi muṭiyumāṟuṅ mēṟkūṟiya yakaram vēṟupaṭa muṭiyumāṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) oṉṟeṉṉu meṇṇiṉ tiripākiya oru eṉpataṉ muṉṉar uyirmutaṉmoḻiyum yāmutaṉmoḻiyum varumoḻi yāy varuṅkālattu am mutaṉilaiyiṉ taṉmaiyāvatu okaram ōkāramāy nīḷum, rakarattu mēl niṉṟa ukaram muṟṟakkeṭṭu ōr eṉa muṭiyumeṉpatām.
(u-m)
ōraṭai, ōrāṭai, ilai, uram, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oḻuṅku, ōlai, auviyam, ōryāṉai eṉavarum.
`tuvara eṉṟataṉāṉ iraṇṭu eṉṉum eṇṇum mūṉṟu eṉṉum vaṇṇum ceyyuḷakattu muṟaiyē īr, īracai, īryāṉai eṉavum mū, mūvacai, mūyāṉai yeṉavum mutal nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭital koḷḷappaṭum.
		iraṇṭumuta loṉpāṉ iṟuti muṉṉar
vaḻaṅkiyal māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ
makara aḷavoṭu nikaralu murittē.			(tol. 480)
(i-ḷ) iraṇṭeṉṉu meṇ mutalāka oṉpatīṟāka niṉṟa eṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉar vaḻakkiṉ kaṇṇē naṭanta aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuriya māveṉṉuñ col varumoḻiyāyvariṉ avveṇṇup peyarkaḷiṉ muṉṉart tantu puṇarkkappaṭum maṇṭai yeṉṉum makaramutal aḷavuppeyarōṭottu vikārap paṭutalu muṭaittu eṉpatām.
ummaiyāṉ vikārappaṭātu iyalpāy muṭitalu muritteṉak koḷka.
(u-m)
irumā, mummā, nāṉmā, aimmā, aṟumā, eḻumā, eṇmā, oṉpatiṉmā eṉavarum. iraṇṭumā, mūṉṟumā eṉavum varum.
vilaṅkumāvai nīkkutaṟku vaḻaṅkiyaṉ mā eṉṟār. ic cūttirattu iraṇṭu mutal oṉpāṉ eṉṟu eṭuttamaiyiṉ oṉṟeṉ pataṉ muṉṉar orumā eṉṉum muṭipēyaṉṟi oṉṟumā eṉṉum muṭipu illaiyāyiṟṟu. mikka eṇṇōṭu kuṟainta eṇ varuṅkāl ummait tokaiyākavum kuṟainta eṇṇōṭu mikka eṇvaruṅ kāl paṇputtokai yākavum muṭittal āciriyar karuttākum.
		laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi yiṟutimuṉ
ummuṅ keḻuvum uḷappaṭap piṟavum
aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻiyiṭait tōṉṟic
ceyyuṭ ṭoṭarvayiṉ meypeṟa nilaiyum
vēṟṟumai kuṟitta poruḷvayi ṉāṉa.			(tol. 481)
itu, lakāra vīṟṟuc ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, itu puḷḷimayaṅkiyaluḷ oḻintu niṉṟa ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟukiṉṟatu eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar.
(i-ḷ) lakāra ṉakāramākiya puḷḷiyīṟṟuc coṉmuṉ um, keḻu eṉṉuñ cāriyaikaḷum piṟa cāriyaikaḷum appeṟṟip paṭṭa moḻiyiṭattē tōṉṟi, ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷait toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟumiṭattu vēṟṟumaiyaik kuṟitta poruṭ puṇarccikkaṇ poruḷpeṟa niṟkum eṉpatām.
(u-m)
vāṉavarivillun tiṅkaḷum, kalkeḻukāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē eṉavum, `mānitikkiḻavaṉum pōṉm eṉavum kāṉ keḻunāṭu eṉavum varum.
tuṟaikeḻumāntai, vaḷaṅkeḻu tirunakar eṉap piṟa īṟuḷḷum iccāriyai varutal `moḻiyiṭait tōṉṟi eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
aṉṉa marapiṉ eṉṟataṉāl cāriyai kāraṇamākap pūkkēḻūraṉ, vaḷaṅkēḻ tirunakar eṉa valleḻuttup peṟutalum nilaimoḻiyīṟu tiritalum cāriyaiyatu ukarakkēṭum ekaranīṭciyum koḷḷappeṟum.
`cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṭ piṟacāriyai peṟṟu vikāraṅkaḷ eyti muṭivaṉavaṟṟiṟ kellām iccūttiramē vitiyāka muṭittuk koḷka eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
		uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yākik
kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum icaiyiṉun tōṉṟi
neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷaviyum
uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai āyiru maruṅkiṉ
aimpā laṟiyum paṇputoku moḻiyuñ
ceyyuñ ceyta eṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyiṉ
meyyoruṅ kiyalun toḻiltoku moḻiyum
tammiyal kiḷappiṉ tammuṉ tāmvarūum
eṇṇiṉ tokuti uḷappaṭap piṟavum
aṉṉavai yellām maruviṉ pāttiya
puṇariyal nilaiyiṭai uṇarat tōṉṟā.	 	(tol. 482)
mēl nilaimoḻiyum varumoḻiyumākac coṟkaḷaip puṇarkkum viti kūṟiya āciriyar, iccūttirattāl aṅṅaṉam niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ ivai yeṉpataṉai eṭutturaikkiṉṟār.
(i-ḷ) uyireḻuttum puḷḷiyeḻuttum īṟāka niṟpatōr collākik kuṟippiṉkaṇṇum icaiyiṉ kaṇṇum paṇpiṉ kaṇṇuntōṉṟi oruvaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmai kuṟainta coṟkaḷākiya uriccoṟkaḷum, oruvaṉ, orutti, palar, oṉṟu, pala eṉṉum aintupālaiyum aṟiyavarum paṇput tokai moḻikaḷum, ceyyum, ceyta eṉṉum peyareccac coṟkaḷiṉuṭaiya kālaṅ kāṭṭum ummum akaramum orucēra naṭakkum puṭai peyarcci tokkuniṟkuñ coṟkaḷum, tamatu taṉmai kūṟumiṭattu niṟuttacollum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumāy vārātu taṉmuṉṉart tāmē vantu niṟkum eṇṇuppeyariṉatu tokutiyum uḷappaṭa attaṉmaiyavākiya piṟavumellām ulakattu maruvinaṭanta vaḻakkiṉatu pakutiyait tam ilakkaṇa mākavuṭaiyaṉavām; avai oṉṟaṉōṭoṉṟu puṇarutal naṭanta taṉmai iṭam viḷaṅkat tōṉṟaveṉṟavāṟu.
uriccol kuṟaiccoṟkiḷaviyātalāṉum, karuñ cāṉṟāṉ eṉap paṇputtokaiyāyavaḻik kariyāṉ cāṉṟāṉ eṉa marūuvāyt tirintu niṉṟatākalāṉum, kolyāṉaiyeṉa viṉaittokaiyāyavaḻik koṉṟa, kolkiṉṟa, kollum eṉak kālaṅkāṭṭuvaṉa kol eṉat tirintaṉavākalāṉum, tammuṉ tām varumeṇṇup peyarkaḷ niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum aṉmaiyāṉum iṅṅaṉam vaḻakkiṭattu marūuvāy vantaṉa niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyumākap puṇarkkappaṭāveṉa āciriyar vilakkiṉār.
(u-m)
viṇviṇaittatu, kārkaṟuttatu, ollolittatu eṉavum karumpārppaṉi eṉavum, kolyāṉai eṉavum pappattu eṉavum varum .
		kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirinavum
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirinavum
viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ
vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅki ṉuṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
naṉmati nāṭṭat teṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 483)
iḵtu ivvatikārap puṟaṉaṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ) muṉṉar eṭuttōtātaṉavāyc ceyyuḷiṭattut tirintu varuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum, vaḻakkiṭattē marūuvāyt tirintu varuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum, muṟkūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇa muṭipiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟiṉ nallaṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉ vaḻakki yaṟkaṇ avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭṭai aṟintu naṭattukaveṉak kūṟuvar pulavar.
(u-m)
taṭa eṉṉum akaravīṟṟuriccol `taṭavuttirai eṉa ukaramum valleḻuttum peṟṟum `taṭavu nilai eṉa ukaram peṟṟum vantatu atu eṉṉum akara vīṟu `atavattukkaṉi eṉa vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attuy peṟṟatu. kacatapa - tōṉṟiṉ eṉpatu `kacatapattōṉṟiṉ eṉa valleḻuttup peṟṟatu.
naṟā, kurā eṉavarum ākāra vīṟṟuccoṟkaḷ `naṟavaṅ kaṇṇi `kuravanīṭiya eṉak `kuṟiyataṉiṟutic ciṉai keṭṭu iru vaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa. ākāra vīṟu piṇā+nāy=piṇavu nāy eṉa alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟātum, irā+vaḻaṅku= iravaḻaṅku eṉa ukaram peṟātum vantaṉa. kaḷḷi+kāṭu= kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu eṉa ikaravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ am cāriyai peṟṟatu. tī+aṉṉa = tīyiṉaṉṉa (malaipaṭu-145) eṉa īkāravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ iṉcāriyaipeṟṟatu. tiru+avar= tiruvat tavar (nālaṭi 57) e ukaravīṟu vēṟṟumaik kaṇ attuc cāriyai peṟṟatu. ē+peṟṟa = ēppeṟṟa (cīvaka-2965) eṉa ēkāravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ekaram peṟātu vantatu. kai+uṇṭām pōḻtē= kaittuṇṭām pōḻtē (nālaṭi-19) eṉavum, kai+ illar=kaittillar (nāṉmaṇi - 69) eṉavum, puṉṉai+kāṉal = puṉṉaiyaṅkāṉal eṉavum, mullai+toṭaiyal = mullaiyantoṭaiyal eṉavum aikāravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attum ammum peṟṟaṉa. kō+il= kōyil eṉa ōkāravīṟu yakaravuṭampaṭu mey peṟṟatu. `kāretir kāṉampāṭiṉēmāka (puṟam 144) `poṉṉan tikiri eṉpuḻik kāṉ, poṉ eṉṉum ṉakara vīṟukaḷ iruvaḻiyum ammup peṟṟaṉa. `vēr piṇi vetirattuk kāl poru naralicai (naṟṟiṇai-62) eṉpuḻi vetir eṉṉum rakaravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ attup peṟṟatu. `nāvalan taṇpoḻil (perumpāṇ-465) eṉa lakaravīṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu. neytal+ciṟupaṟai= neytalañ ciṟupaṟai eṉa lakara vīṟu alvaḻikkaṇ ammup peṟṟatu, āyiṭai yirupērāṇmai ceytapūcal (kuṟuntokai - 43) eṉpuḻi `av eṉṉum vakara vīṟu `iṭai eṉṉum urupoṭu puṇarntu `āyiṭai eṉa nīṇṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu. tev+muṉai=temmuṉai eṉpuḻi vakaraṅ keṭṭu makara voṟṟuppeṟṟu muṭintatu. a+aṉaittum = aṉṟi yaṉaittum eṉa akarac cuṭṭu aṉṟi eṉat tirintatu. kōṅkiṉ mukai, teṅkiṉpaḻam eṉak kuṟṟukara vīṟu iṉpeṟṟaṉa. ivvāṟē ceyyuḷil tirintu varuvaṉavuḷavēl avaṟṟai yellām ivvatikārap puṟaṉaṭaiyāl muṭittuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
`arumaruntaṉṉāṉ eṉaṟpālatu arumaruntāṉ eṉa marūvāy muṭintatu eṉa iḷampūraṇarum, `aru maruntāṉ eṉpatu rakara vukaraṅ keṭṭu arumantāṉ eṉa muṭintatu  eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar. cōḻaṉ + nāṭu = cōṇāṭu eṉavum, pāṇṭiyaṉ + nāṭu = pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉavum aṉ keṭṭu muṭintaṉa. toṇṭaimāṉ + nāṭu = toṇṭai nāṭu eṉa īṟṟeḻuttuc cila keṭṭum, malaiyamāṉ +nāṭu = malāṭu eṉa mutaleḻut toḻintaṉa palavuṅ keṭṭum muṭintaṉa eṉavum, potu + il = potiyil eṉa  ukarantirintu ikaramāy yakaravuṭampaṭumey peṟṟu muṭintatu eṉavum, piṟavum ivvāṟē tirintu maruvi vaḻaṅkuvaṉa vellām ippuṟaṉaṭai yāṉ amaittuk koḷka eṉavum viti kūṟuvar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.
naṉṉūlāciriyarākiya pavaṇanti muṉivar tām vakuttuk koṇṭa vikutip puṇarcci, patap puṇarcci, cāriyaip puṇarcci, urupu puṇarcci ākiya nāṉkiṉ puṇarccik kaṇṇum vēṟupaṭa vantavaṟṟai yellām uṇarntu oppunōkki amaittuk koḷḷum muṟaiyil puṟaṉaṭaic cūttiraṅkaḷākac cila nūṟpākkaḷai eḻuttatikārattiṉ iṟutiyil amaittuḷḷaṉar.
		itaṟkitu cāriyai yeṉiṉ aḷaviṉmaiyiṉ
vikutiyum patamum urupum pakuttu, iṭai
niṉṟa eḻuttum patamum iyaṟkaiyum
oṉṟa uṇarttal uravōr neṟiyē.			   (naṉ. 253)
eṉpatu cāriyai paṟṟiya puṟaṉaṭaiyākum.
vikuti mutaliya puṇarccikkaṇ iṉṉataṟku iṉṉa cāriyai eṉṟu varaiyaṟuttu vitittaṉa vaṟṟuḷḷum oḻintaṉa vaṟṟuḷḷum taṉittaṉi yeṭuttuk kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avai aḷaviṉṟi viriyumākaliṉ vikutip puṇarcciyiṉaiyum patappuṇarcci yiṉaiyum urupupuṇarcciyiṉaiyum kaṇṭaviṭattu avaṟṟaip pakuttu avaṟṟiṉiṭaiyē niṉṟa ēkāram akaram mutaliya eḻuttuc cāriyaiyiṉaiyum aṉ āṉ mutaliya pataccāriyai yiṉaiyum ivvirucāriyaiyum peṟātu iyalpāy niṉṟa taṉmaiyiṉaiyum teḷiya aṟivittal aṟiñaratu neṟi muṟai yākum eṉpatu innūṟpāviṉ poruḷākum.
urupu puṇarccikkaṇ vititta cāriyai mutaliya muṭipukaḷ av vuruputokka poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇṇum uriyavai yuriyaṉa eṉpatu,
		urupiṉ muṭiyavai okkumap poruḷiṉum
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttappaṭṭatu.			   (naṉ. 238)
pavaṇanti muṉivar tām iyaṟṟiya naṉṉūl karukka nūlātaliṉ ataṉkaṇ vikuti, patam, cāriyai, urupu eṉṉum nāṉkiṉ puṇarccikkaṇṇum vitikaḷ potuppaṭak kūṟap paṭṭaṉavāyiṉum avvitikaḷuḷ ivviti itaṟkup poruntum ivviti itaṟkup  poruntātu eṉa uyttuṇarntu ivviti itaṟkup poruntātu eṉa uyttuṇarntu evviti etaṟkup poruntumō avvitiyai ataṟkuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum eṉa aṟivuṟuttum muṟaiyil,
		vikutipatañ cāriyai yurupaṉaittiṉum
uraitta vitiyiṉōrn toppaṉa koḷalē.		   (naṉ. 265)
eṉap puṟaṉaṭai kūṟiyuḷḷamai āḻntuṇarat takkatākum. ic cūttiravuraiyil naṉṉū luraiyāciriyarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya viḷakkaṅkaḷ kūrntuṇarttakkaṉavākum.
		iyalpiṉ vikāramum vikārat tiyalpum
uyartiṇai yiṭattu virintun tokkum
viravup peyariṉ virintu niṉṟum
aṉṉa piṟavu mākum aiyurupē.
eṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaikkum,
		puḷḷiyum uyiru māyiṟu coṉmuṉ
tammiṉākiya toḻiṉmoḻi variṉē
valliṉam vikaṟpamum iyalpum ākum.			   (naṉ. 256)
eṉa mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaikkum amainta puṟaṉaṭaic cūttiraṅkaḷum ivvatikārattuḷ itaṟku itu muṭipeṉṟu vitikkat takuvaṉa vaṟṟaiyellām taṉittaṉi varaiyaṟuttu vitikūṟat toṭaṅkiṉ avai varam piṉṟi viriyumātalāl vakuttu viti kūṟiyavaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷai ētuvākak koṇṭu vakuttu viti kūṟāta vaṟṟaiyum karutalaḷavaiyāl vakuttu viti yuraittukkoḷka eṉa aṟivuṟuttum muṟaiyil
		itaṟkitu muṭipeṉ ṟeñcā tiyāvum
vitippaḷa viṉmaiyiṉ vitittavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ
vakutturai yātavum vakuttaṉar koḷalē.		   (naṉ. 257)
eṉap pavaṇanti muṉivar kūṟiya atikārap puṟaṉaṭaiyum mēṟkāṭṭiya nūṟpākkaḷukku uraiyāciriyarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷum viḷakkaṅkaḷum eḻuttatikārattiṟku amainta puṟaṉaṭai vitikaḷāka iṅku oppunōkki yuṇarntu koḷḷat takkaṉavākum.
		kūṟiya kuṉṟiṉum mutaṉūl kūṭṭit
tōmiṉ ṟuṇartal tollāp piyaṉṟaṉ
āṇaiyiṟ ṟamiḻaṟin tōrkkuk kaṭaṉē.
eṉṟār palkāppiyaṉārum.
	piṟcērkkai
uyireḻuttukkaḷil kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷukku neṭṭeḻut tukkaḷ piṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟāl otta oliyamaippuṭaiyaṉa eṉpatum, otta kuṟṟeḻuttillāta aikāra aukāraṅkaḷukku muṟaiyē ikara ukaraṅkaḷ icaiyāl ottaṉa eṉpatum,
		kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikkum
neṭṭeḻut timpar ottakuṟ ṟeḻuttē.
		ai au veṉṉum āyī reḻuttiṟku
ikara ukaram icainiṟai vākum.		         (tol. 41, 42)
eṉavarum nūṟpākkaḷāl uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟē kacaṭatapaṟa eṉṉum valleḻuttāṟukkum ṅañaṇanamaṉa eṉṉum melleḻut tāṟum muṟaiyē ottup poruntum ocai yuṭaiyaṉa ātalāl avaṟṟaik kiḷaiyoṟṟu eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar tolkāppiyaṉār. ivviru cūttiraṅkaḷaiyum aṭiyoṟṟi,
		ai au iuc ceṟiya mutaleḻuttu
ivviraṇṭu ōriṉamāy varal muṟaiyē.		     (naṉ. 71)
eṉa aā, iī, kaṅa, caña eṉṟāṅku eḻuttukkaḷai iṉappaṭut tiṉār naṉṉūlār,
		tāṉam, muyaṟci, aḷavu, poruḷ, vaṭivu
āṉa voṉṟāti ōr puṭaiyoppu iṉamē.		     (naṉ. 72)
eṉa iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇamuṅ kūṟiṉār.
eḻuttukkaḷukkup piṟappuṇarttum nilaiyil uyireḻut tukkaḷuḷḷum, meyyeḻuttukkaḷuḷḷum pala eḻuttuk kaḷukkuc cērttup piṟappu oṉṟākac collappaṭṭaṉa vēṉum avai taṉittaṉi eḻuttātaṟkēṟṟa olivēṟupāṭuṭaiyaṉa eṉa nuṇuki yuṇartal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉai,
		tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.				 (tol. 88)
eṉavarum piṟappiyaṟ cūttirattil āciriyar tol kāppiyaṉār kuṟittuḷḷār. avar kuṟittavaṇṇa eḻuttuk kaḷiṭaiyē yamainta ciṟiya vēṟṟumaikkuriya oli muyaṟciyākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷai,
		eṭuttal paṭuttal nalita luḻappiṟ
ṟiriyum tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum.			     (naṉ. 88)
eṉavarum cūttirattil paṇavantiyār teḷivākak kuṟit tuḷḷamai kāṇalām.
moḻikaḷ tammuṭpuṇaruṅkāl ōreḻuttu niṟṟaṟkuriya nilaiyil maṟṟō reḻuttup pōliyāy niṟṟaluṇṭu eṉpataṉai,
		ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvu ñāvum
viṉaiyō raṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.			(tol. 146)
eṉpataṉāl uṇarttiṉār tolkāppiyar. ivviti yiṉai iṟantatuvilakkal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟip pavaṇantiyār kūṟātu viṭṭaṉarāyiṉum, ivvāṟu moḻiyiṉ mutalilum iṭaiyilum payiṉṟuvarum eḻuttup pōlikaḷai etiratu pōṟṟal eṉṉum uttiyāl naṉṉūl 123, 124-ām cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ kuṟittuḷḷār.
cila viṭaṅkaḷil moḻi mutalilum iṭaiyilum akaram niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattu aikāram pōliyāy varumeṉpataṉaiyum yuṇarttuvatu,
		a ai mutaliṭai yokkuñ cañayamuṉ.			   (naṉ. 123)
eṉṟa cūttiramākum. u-m. mayal maiyal eṉavum aracu araicu eṉavum varum.
cila iṭaṅkaḷil aikārattiṉ piṉṉum yakaravoṟṟiṉ piṉṉum iyalpāy nakaram niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattu ñakaram pōliyāy varutaluṇṭeṉpatu,
ceyññiṉṟa nīla malarkiṉṟa tillaicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
maiññiṉṟa voṇkaṇ malaimakaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻntuniṟka
neyññiṉ ṟeriyum viḷakkotta nīla maṇimiṭaṟṟāṉ
kaiññiṉṟa āṭalkaṇ ṭāṟpiṉṉaik kaṇkoṇṭu kāṇpateṉṉē.
eṉat tirunāvukkaracar aruḷiya tiruviruttattāl aṟiyappaṭum. ivvāṟu cey + niṉṟa = ceynniṉṟa, ceyñ ñiṉṟa eṉavum, mai+niṉṟa=mainniṉṟa maiññiṉṟa eṉavum puṇar moḻiyiṭai varum pōliyaip piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟi,
		aikāṉ yavvaḻi navvoṭu cilvaḻi
ñaḵkā ṉuṟaḻu meṉmarum uḷarē.			   (naṉ. 124)
eṉṟa cūttirattāṟ kuṟittār pavaṇanti muṉivar.
taṉatu toḻilaic collavarum ākāravīṟṟu viṉā viṉai yuṭaiya viṉaiccollum, yāveṉṉum viṉāp peyarum, viḷittalaiyuṭaiya peyarākiya ākāravīṟṟu uyar tiṇaic collum valleḻuttu mutaliya vantu puṇaruṅkāl iyalpāy muṭivaṉa eṉa `āvumāvum viḷippeyark kiḷaviyum eṉavarum 224 ām cūttirattiṟ kuṟittār tolkāppiyaṉār. itaṉai,
		īṟṟiyā viṉāviḷip peyarmuṉ valipiyalpē.		   (naṉ. 160)
eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ pavaṇanti muṉivar kuṟittuḷḷār.
illāmai eṉṉum kuṟippuṇarttum ilam eṉṉum uriccoṟkumuṉ uṇṭātal eṉṉum poruḷuṭaiya `paṭu eṉṉum viṉaikkuṟippup peyar varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattuc ceyyuḷiṭattu makarak kēṭum tiripumiṉṟi `ilampaṭu eṉa iyalpāy niṟkum. itaṉai yuṇarttuvatu,
		ilameṉ kiḷavikkup paṭuvaru kālai
nilaiyalu murittē ceyyu ḷāṉa.			(tol. 316)
eṉṟa nūṟpā. itaṉai innūliṉ 256-ām pakkattuc cērkka.
makkaḷ eṉṉum peyarccolliṟuti ṉakaram iyal pātalēyaṉṟit takka iṭam aṟintu valloṟṟākat tirintu makkaṭkai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavarutalum uritteṉak kūṟuvatu,
		makka ḷeṉṉum peyarcco liṟuti
takkavaḻi yaṟintu valittalu murittē.			(tol.404)
eṉṟa nūṟpāvākum itaṉai 253-ām pakkattil tirintu muṭiyum īṟukaḷ eṉṟa talaippiṉkīḻc cērkka.
av, iv, uv eṉṉum vakara īṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarkaḷiṉ vakaram alvaḻikkaṇ valleḻuttu mutaṉ moḻivariṉ (av+kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya eṉṟāṅku) āytamākat tirintu varum eṉpataṉai yuṇarttum.
		vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi yāyta mākum.			(tol. 379)
eṉṟa cūttirattiṉaiyum, vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭiṉ tiripuṇarttiya,
		melleḻut tiyaiyiṉ avveḻut tākum.		(tol. 380)
eṉṟa cūttirattaiyum 256-ām pakkat toṭakkattiṟ cērttuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
